    }

    // ── Models：服务商列表（52px 行）→ 详情（返回 / Wrench / 启用）→ 高级设置（自定义请求头）──
    // 几何逐值取自原版只读源码 `features/settings/ModelSettings.tsx`、
    // `features/settings/ProviderAdvanced.tsx` 与 `styles/app.css`：
    //   `.cf-pane` 内边距 16px 20px；`.models__head` 28px + 下间距 16px；
    //   `.provider-row` min-height:52px、padding:10px 14px、gap:12px；
    //   `.provider-row__dot` 10×10；`.provider-row__sub` 11.5px / fg-subtle；
    //   `.provider-detail__topbar` 30px + 下间距 16px；`.provider-detail__head` 名称 18px、
    //     下边框上方留 16px；`.provider-detail__name-input` 只用于自定义服务商；
    //   `.field` 下间距 20px；`.field__row-between` 下间距 8px；`.model-list` 1px 边框、radius 8；
    //   `.model-row` 44px、padding 0 12px、gap 8px；`.header-item__name` 占 34%。
    // 数据只走已入库的 `mvvm.services.settings_models` 投影：`modelRows()` /
    // `modelsPaneLabels()` / `providerDetail()`，写回只走 `setProviderHeaders()` /
    // `setProviderField()`；QML 不新建数据源，也不在界面重算保留头规则。
    Component {
        id: modelsPane
        Item {
            id: modPane
            property var rows: shellBridge ? shellBridge.modelRows() : []
            property var text: shellBridge ? shellBridge.modelsPaneLabels() : ({})
            // 列表 / 详情 / 高级设置三态；page=settings-models-* 时由 Main.qml 指定初始态。
            property string view: "list"
            property string selectedId: ""
            property var detail: ({})
            property var headerRows: []


            function applyInitialView() {
                if (root.modelsView === "list") {
                    view = "list"
                    return
                }
                if (rows.length > 0) {
                    openProvider(rows[0].id)
                    if (root.modelsView === "advanced") view = "advanced"
                }
            }

            Component.onCompleted: applyInitialView()

            // qml_shell 在 QML 加载完成后才写 `page`；监听 modelsView 的后续变化，
            // 截图页才能从默认 list 切到 provider / advanced。
            Connections {
                target: root
                function onModelsViewChanged() { modPane.applyInitialView() }
            }

            function refreshRows() {
                rows = shellBridge ? shellBridge.modelRows() : []
            }

            function openProvider(providerId) {
                selectedId = providerId
                reloadDetail()
                view = "detail"
            }

            function requestDelete() {
                if (selectedId === "") return
                root.providerDeleteName = detail.name || ""
                root.providerDeleteOpen = true
            }

            function confirmDelete() {
                if (!shellBridge || selectedId === "") {
                    root.providerDeleteOpen = false
                    return
                }
                var ok = shellBridge.deleteProvider(selectedId)
                root.providerDeleteOpen = false
                if (ok) backToList()
            }

            function reloadDetail() {
                detail = shellBridge ? shellBridge.providerDetail(selectedId) : ({})
                var list = (detail && detail.headers) ? detail.headers : []
                var next = []
                for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
                    next.push({
                        name: list[i].name || "",
                        value: list[i].value || "",
                        issueText: list[i].issueText || ""
                    })
                }
                headerRows = next
            }

            function openAdvanced() {
                reloadDetail()
                view = "advanced"
            }

            function backToList() {
                view = "list"
                selectedId = ""
                detail = ({})
                headerRows = []
                refreshRows()
            }

            function persistHeaders() {
                if (!shellBridge || selectedId === "") return
                var out = []
                for (var i = 0; i < headerRows.length; i++) {
                    out.push({
                        name: headerRows[i].name || "",
                        value: headerRows[i].value || ""
                    })
                }
                shellBridge.setProviderHeaders(selectedId, out)
            }

            function addHeader() {
                var next = []
                for (var i = 0; i < headerRows.length; i++) next.push(headerRows[i])
                next.push({ name: "", value: "", issueText: "" })
                headerRows = next
                headerSaveTimer.restart()
            }

            function removeHeader(index) {
                if (index < 0 || index >= headerRows.length) return
                var next = []
                for (var i = 0; i < headerRows.length; i++) {
                    if (i !== index) next.push(headerRows[i])
                }
                headerRows = next
                persistHeaders()
            }

            function editHeader(index, field, value) {
                if (index < 0 || index >= headerRows.length) return
                // 数组元素是 JS 对象；原地改，不重绑整个 Repeater（否则输入框会丢焦点）。
                headerRows[index][field] = value
                headerRows[index].issueText = ""
                headerSaveTimer.restart()
            }

            Timer {
                id: headerSaveTimer
                interval: 350
                repeat: false
                onTriggered: modPane.persistHeaders()
            }

            // ── 列表态 ────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Item {
                id: modelsListView
                anchors.fill: parent
                visible: modPane.view === "list"

                Item {
                    id: modHead
                    x: 20
                    y: 16
                    width: parent.width - 40
                    height: 28

                    Text {
                        id: modTitle
                        anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                        text: modPane.text.providers || ""
                        color: Theme.fg
                        font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                        font.pixelSize: 15
                        font.weight: 700
                    }

                    Rectangle {
                        id: addBtn
                        objectName: "modelsAddProvider"
                        anchors.right: parent.right
                        width: 28
                        height: 28
                        radius: Theme.radius
                        color: addHover.hovered ? Theme.bgHover : Theme.bgElevated
                        border.width: 1
                        border.color: Theme.border
                        Icon {
                            anchors.centerIn: parent
                            name: IconData.plus
                            size: 16
                            color: addHover.hovered ? Theme.fg : Theme.fgMuted
                        }
                        HoverHandler { id: addHover }
                        // 原版 `onAddProvider('llm')`：新建自定义服务商后直接进其详情
                        // （`ModelSettings.tsx:223-230`；`addProvider` 返回新 id）。
                        MouseArea {
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onClicked: {
                                if (!shellBridge) return
                                var newId = shellBridge.addProvider()
                                if (!newId) return
                                modPane.refreshRows()
                                modPane.openProvider(newId)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    SettingsButton {
                        objectName: "modelsCustomNetwork"
                        anchors.right: addBtn.left
                        anchors.rightMargin: 8
                        height: 28
                        text: "自定义模型网络"
                        onClicked: root.pane = "custom-model"
                    }
                }

                Rectangle {
                    id: modRows
                    // 与标题/加号同处 `.cf-pane` 内容列（pane x=20、宽 pane-40）：
                    // 原版 `.models__rows` 实测外框 510×54，左边框 x=469、右边框 x=978。
                    x: 20
                    y: 60   // 16（上内边距）+ 28（head）+ 16（`.models__head` 下间距）
                    width: parent.width - 40
                    height: modPane.rows.length === 0
                            ? modEmpty.implicitHeight + 32
                            : modPane.rows.length * 52 + 2
                    radius: Theme.radiusLg
                    color: "transparent"
                    border.width: 1
                    border.color: Theme.border
                    clip: true

                    Text {
                        id: modEmpty
                        visible: modPane.rows.length === 0
                        x: 1
                        y: 17
                        width: parent.width - 2
                        horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
                        text: modPane.text.noProvidersYet || ""
                        color: Theme.fgMuted
                        font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                        font.pixelSize: 13
                    }

                    ListView {
                        id: modList
                        visible: modPane.rows.length > 0
                        x: 1
                        y: 1
                        width: parent.width - 2
                        height: parent.height - 2
                        clip: true
                        model: modPane.rows
                        delegate: Rectangle {
                            id: providerRow
                            required property var modelData
                            required property int index

                            width: modList.width
                            height: 52
                            color: providerMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.bgHover : "transparent"

                            // `.provider-row + .provider-row`：只在行与行之间画 1px 顶线。
                            Rectangle {
                                visible: providerRow.index > 0
                                anchors.top: parent.top
                                width: parent.width
                                height: 1
                                color: Theme.border
                            }

                            // `.provider-row__dot`：启用绿点 / 未启用灰点。
                            Rectangle {
                                x: 14
                                anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                                width: 10
                                height: 10
                                radius: 5
                                color: providerRow.modelData.enabled === true ? Theme.success : Theme.fgSubtle
                            }

                            Column {
                                id: providerMain
                                anchors.left: parent.left
                                anchors.leftMargin: 36   // 14 padding + 10 dot + 12 gap
                                anchors.right: providerAside.left
                                anchors.rightMargin: 12
                                anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                                spacing: 2

                                Text {
                                    width: parent.width
                                    text: providerRow.modelData.name || ""
                                    color: Theme.fg
                                    font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                                    font.pixelSize: 13
                                    font.weight: 600
                                    elide: Text.ElideRight
                                }
                                Text {
                                    width: parent.width
                                    text: providerRow.modelData.detail || ""
                                    color: Theme.fgMuted
                                    font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                                    // `.provider-row__sub`（`app.css:4567-4573`）：font-size:11.5px。
                                    // pixelSize 只收整数，半像素用 pointSize 表达（96dpi 下
                                    // 11.5px = 8.625pt），与 Main.qml:1351 的 13.5px 同一做法。
                                    font.pointSize: 8.625
                                    elide: Text.ElideRight
                                }
                            }

                            Row {
                                id: providerAside
                                anchors.right: parent.right
                                anchors.rightMargin: 14
                                anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                                spacing: 8

                                // `.provider-row__aside`（`app.css:4574-4580`）是 `align-items:center`：
                                // 14px 告警三角在 16px 行高里垂直居中，故下移 1px。QML 的 Row
                                // 只排 x、不动 y，不加这一行三角会比 chevron 高 1px（实测 deva
                                // 三角上沿 y=265、改前 QML 为 264）。
                                Icon {
                                    visible: providerRow.modelData.warn === true
                                    y: 1
                                    name: IconData.alertTriangle
                                    size: 14
                                    color: Theme.warning
                                }
                                Icon {
                                    name: IconData.chevronRight
                                    size: 16
                                    color: Theme.fgSubtle
                                }
                            }

                            MouseArea {
                                id: providerMouse
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                hoverEnabled: true
                                cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                                onClicked: modPane.openProvider(providerRow.modelData.id)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // ── 详情态 ────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Item {
                id: detailView
                x: 20
                y: 16
                width: parent.width - 40
                height: parent.height - 32
                visible: modPane.view === "detail"

                Item {
                    id: detailTopbar
                    width: parent.width
                    height: 30

                    Item {
                        id: detailBack
                        anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                        width: detailBackRow.width + 16
                        height: 24

                        Rectangle {
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            radius: Theme.radiusSm
                            color: detailBackMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.bgHover : "transparent"
                        }
                        Row {
                            id: detailBackRow
                            x: 6
                            anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                            spacing: 4
                            Icon {
                                name: IconData.chevronLeft
                                size: 16
                                color: detailBackMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.fg : Theme.fgMuted
                            }
                            Text {
                                text: modPane.text.back || ""
                                color: detailBackMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.fg : Theme.fgMuted
                                font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                                font.pixelSize: 13
                            }
                        }
                        MouseArea {
                            id: detailBackMouse
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            hoverEnabled: true
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onClicked: modPane.backToList()
                        }
                    }

                    Row {
                        id: detailActions
                        anchors.right: parent.right
                        anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                        spacing: 12

                        // `.provider-detail__del`（`app.css:4905`）：官方与自定义服务商都可删，
                        // 点按弹确认（原版 `onDeleteProvider`，`ModelSettings.tsx:195`）。
                        Rectangle {
                            id: detailDelete
                            objectName: "providerDelete"
                            visible: modPane.selectedId !== ""
                            width: 30
                            height: 30
                            radius: Theme.radiusSm
                            color: deleteMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.bgHover : "transparent"
                            Icon {
                                anchors.centerIn: parent
                                name: IconData.trash2
                                size: 15
                                color: deleteMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.danger : Theme.fgSubtle
                            }
                            MouseArea {
                                id: deleteMouse
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                hoverEnabled: true
                                cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                                onClicked: modPane.requestDelete()
                            }
                        }

                        Rectangle {
                            id: detailWrench
                            objectName: "providerWrench"
                            visible: modPane.detail.advanced === true
                            width: 30
                            height: 30
                            radius: Theme.radiusSm
                            color: wrenchMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.bgHover : "transparent"
                            Icon {
                                anchors.centerIn: parent
                                name: IconData.wrench
                                size: 15
                                color: wrenchMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.fg : Theme.fgMuted
                            }
                            MouseArea {
                                id: wrenchMouse
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                hoverEnabled: true
                                cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                                onClicked: modPane.openAdvanced()
                            }
                        }

                        Text {
                            text: modPane.text.enableProvider || modPane.text.enabled || ""
                            height: 30
                            verticalAlignment: Text.AlignVCenter
                            color: Theme.fgMuted
                            font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                            font.pixelSize: 12
                        }

                        Item {
                            id: detailSwitch
                            width: 36
                            height: 20
                            anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter

                            Rectangle {
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                radius: 10
                                color: modPane.detail.enabled === true ? Theme.accent : Theme.bgActive
                            }
                            Rectangle {
                                x: modPane.detail.enabled === true ? 18 : 2
                                y: 2
                                width: 16
                                height: 16
                                radius: 8
                                color: Theme.switchThumb
                            }
                            MouseArea {
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                                onClicked: {
                                    if (!shellBridge) return
                                    shellBridge.setProviderField(modPane.selectedId, "enabled",
                                                                 modPane.detail.enabled === true ? "0" : "1")
                                    modPane.reloadDetail()
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                Item {
                    id: detailHead
                    y: 46   // topbar 30 + `.provider-detail__topbar` margin-bottom 16
                    width: parent.width
                    height: 38

                    Text {
                        x: 0
                        y: 0
                        width: parent.width
                        text: modPane.detail.name || ""
                        color: Theme.fg
                        font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                        font.pixelSize: 18
                        font.weight: 600
                        elide: Text.ElideRight
                    }
                    Rectangle {
                        anchors.bottom: parent.bottom
                        width: parent.width
                        height: 1
                        color: Theme.border
                    }
                }

                Rectangle {
                    id: detailWarn
                    visible: modPane.detail.warn === true
                    y: detailHead.y + detailHead.height + 12   // head 下间距 20 + `.provider-warn` margin-top -8
                    width: parent.width
                    height: detailWarnRow.implicitHeight + 16
                    radius: Theme.radiusSm
                    color: Qt.rgba(Theme.warning.r, Theme.warning.g, Theme.warning.b, 0.08)
                    border.width: 1
                    border.color: Qt.rgba(Theme.warning.r, Theme.warning.g, Theme.warning.b, 0.4)

                    Row {
                        id: detailWarnRow
                        x: 10
                        y: 8
                        spacing: 6
                        Icon {
                            name: IconData.alertTriangle
                            size: 13
                            color: Theme.warning
                        }
                        Text {
                            text: modPane.text.keyMissingWarn || ""
                            color: Theme.fgMuted
                            font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                            font.pixelSize: 12
                        }
                    }
                }

                Item {
                    id: detailModels
                    y: detailWarn.visible ? detailWarn.y + detailWarn.height + 16
                                             : detailHead.y + detailHead.height + 20
                    width: parent.width
                    height: detailModelsLabel.implicitHeight + 8 + detailModelsBox.height

                    Text {
                        id: detailModelsLabel
                        text: modPane.detail.modelList || ""
                        color: Theme.fg
                        font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                        font.pixelSize: 13
                        font.weight: 600
                    }

                    Rectangle {
                        id: detailModelsBox
                        y: 21   // label 行高约 13 + `.field__row-between` 下间距 8
                        width: parent.width
                        height: {
                            var list = modPane.detail.models || []
                            if (list.length === 0) return 49
                            return list.length * 44 + Math.max(0, list.length - 1) * 1 + 2
                        }
                        radius: Theme.radius
                        color: "transparent"
                        border.width: 1
                        border.color: Theme.border
                        clip: true

                        Text {
                            visible: (modPane.detail.models || []).length === 0
                            x: 1
                            y: 17
                            width: parent.width - 2
                            horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
                            text: modPane.text.noModels || ""
                            color: Theme.fgMuted
                            font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                            font.pixelSize: 13
                        }

                        Column {
                            x: 1
                            y: 1
                            width: parent.width - 2
                            Repeater {
                                model: modPane.detail.models || []
                                delegate: Rectangle {
                                    id: detailModelRow
                                    required property var modelData
                                    required property int index
                                    width: detailModelsBox.width - 2
                                    height: 44
                                    color: "transparent"

                                    Rectangle {
                                        visible: detailModelRow.index < (modPane.detail.models || []).length - 1
                                        anchors.bottom: parent.bottom
                                        width: parent.width
                                        height: 1
                                        color: Theme.border
                                    }

                                    Row {
                                        x: 12
                                        anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                                        spacing: 8
                                        Text {
                                            text: detailModelRow.modelData.name || ""
                                            color: Theme.fg
                                            font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                                            font.pixelSize: 13
                                            font.weight: 500
                                        }
                                        Text {
                                            visible: detailModelRow.modelData.showId === true
                                            text: detailModelRow.modelData.id || ""
                                            color: Theme.fgMuted
                                            font.family: Theme.monoFamily
                                            font.pixelSize: 11
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // ── 高级设置态：自定义请求头 ────────────────────────────────
            Item {
                id: advancedView
                x: 20
                y: 16
                width: parent.width - 40
                height: parent.height - 32
                visible: modPane.view === "advanced"

                Item {
                    id: advancedTopbar
                    width: parent.width
                    height: 30

                    Item {
                        id: advancedBack
                        anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                        width: advancedBackRow.width + 16
                        height: 24

                        Rectangle {
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            radius: Theme.radiusSm
                            color: advancedBackMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.bgHover : "transparent"
                        }
                        Row {
                            id: advancedBackRow
                            x: 6
                            anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                            spacing: 4
                            Icon {
                                name: IconData.chevronLeft
                                size: 16
                                color: advancedBackMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.fg : Theme.fgMuted
                            }
                            Text {
                                text: (modPane.text.backTo || "").replace("{name}", modPane.detail.name || "")
                                color: advancedBackMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.fg : Theme.fgMuted
                                font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                                font.pixelSize: 13
                            }
                        }
                        MouseArea {
                            id: advancedBackMouse
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            hoverEnabled: true
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onClicked: modPane.view = "detail"
                        }
                    }
                }

                Item {
                    id: advancedField
                    y: 46   // topbar 30 + 16
                    width: parent.width
                    height: advancedFieldHead.height + 8 + headerListBox.height + 8
                            + headersHint.implicitHeight

                    Item {
                        id: advancedFieldHead
                        width: parent.width
                        height: 26

                        Text {
                            anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                            text: modPane.text.headers || ""
                            color: Theme.fg
                            font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                            font.pixelSize: 13
                            font.weight: 600
                        }

                        Rectangle {
                            id: addHeaderBtn
                            anchors.right: parent.right
                            anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                            width: addHeaderRow.implicitWidth + 16
                            height: 26
                            radius: Theme.radiusSm
                            color: addHeaderMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.bgHover : "transparent"

                            Row {
                                id: addHeaderRow
                                anchors.centerIn: parent
                                spacing: 8
                                Icon {
                                    name: IconData.plus
                                    size: 14
                                    color: addHeaderMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.fg : Theme.fgMuted
                                }
                                Text {
                                    text: modPane.text.addHeader || ""
                                    color: addHeaderMouse.containsMouse ? Theme.fg : Theme.fgMuted
                                    font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                                    font.pixelSize: 12
                                    font.weight: 500
                                }
                            }
                            MouseArea {
                                id: addHeaderMouse
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                hoverEnabled: true
                                cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                                onClicked: modPane.addHeader()
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Rectangle {
                        id: headerListBox
                        y: 34   // field__row-between 26 + 下间距 8
                        width: parent.width
                        height: {
                            if (modPane.headerRows.length === 0) return 49
                            var issueExtra = 0
                            for (var i = 0; i < modPane.headerRows.length; i++) {
                                if (modPane.headerRows[i].issueText) issueExtra += 18
                            }
                            return modPane.headerRows.length * 44
                                 + Math.max(0, modPane.headerRows.length - 1) * 1
                                 + issueExtra + 2
                        }
                        radius: Theme.radius
                        color: "transparent"
                        border.width: 1
                        border.color: Theme.border
                        clip: true

                        Text {
                            visible: modPane.headerRows.length === 0
                            x: 1
                            y: 17
                            width: parent.width - 2
                            horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
                            text: modPane.text.noHeaders || ""
                            color: Theme.fgMuted
                            font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                            font.pixelSize: 13
                        }

                        Column {
                            x: 1
                            y: 1
                            width: parent.width - 2
                            Repeater {
                                model: modPane.headerRows
                                delegate: Item {
                                    id: headerRow
                                    required property var modelData
                                    required property int index
                                    width: headerListBox.width - 2
                                    height: 44 + (modelData.issueText ? 18 : 0)
                                            + (headerRow.index > 0 ? 1 : 0)

                                    Rectangle {
                                        visible: headerRow.index > 0
                                        anchors.top: parent.top
                                        width: parent.width
                                        height: 1
                                        color: Theme.border
                                    }

                                    Rectangle {
                                        id: headerNameBox
                                        x: 12
                                        y: 6 + (headerRow.index > 0 ? 1 : 0)
                                        width: Math.round((headerRow.width - 24) * 0.34)
                                        height: 32
                                        radius: Theme.radiusSm
                                        color: Theme.bgElevated
                                        border.width: 1
                                        border.color: headerNameInput.activeFocus ? Theme.accent : Theme.borderStrong

                                        Text {
                                            visible: headerNameInput.text.length === 0
                                            x: 12
                                            anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                                            text: modPane.text.headerName || ""
                                            color: Theme.fgMuted
                                            font.family: Theme.sansFamily
                                            font.pixelSize: 13
                                            enabled: false
                                        }
                                        TextInput {
                                            id: headerNameInput
                                            anchors.fill: parent
                                            anchors.leftMargin: 12
                                            anchors.rightMargin: 12
                                            verticalAlignment: TextInput.AlignVCenter

    // ── About：56px 图标 + 名称 + 版本 + 检查更新 ─────────────────────────
    // 几何逐项取自原版：.cf-about padding 24px 0；.cf-about__name margin-top 12px、

    Component {
        id: aboutPane
        Item {
            id: aboutPaneRoot
            objectName: "aboutPaneRoot"
            // 文案与状态都从 Python 投影取；QML 只画。
            property var labels: shellBridge ? shellBridge.modelsPaneLabels() : ({})
            property var updateState: ({phase: "idle"})
            property string updatePhase: "idle"
            property bool checking: updatePhase === "checking"

            function applyUpdateState(state) {
                updateState = state || {phase: "idle"}
                var phase = state ? (state["phase"] || state.phase || "idle") : "idle"
                updatePhase = "" + phase
            }

            function checkUpdates() {
                if (!shellBridge || checking)
                    return
                applyUpdateState({phase: "checking"})
                shellBridge.checkForUpdates()
            }

            Component.onCompleted: {
                if (shellBridge)
                    applyUpdateState(shellBridge.updateState())
            }

            Connections {
                target: shellBridge
                function onUpdateStateChanged(state) { aboutPaneRoot.applyUpdateState(state) }
            }

            // SettingsPane.tsx::AboutPane 先绘制 cf-pane__title，再绘制居中的品牌区。
            Text {
                objectName: "aboutPaneHeading"

$ nl -ba /home/agent/shared_workspace/deva/apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/i18n/i18n.tsx
1: import {
2:   createContext,
3:   useContext,
4:   useEffect,
5:   useMemo,
6:   useState,
7:   type ReactNode
8: } from 'react'
9: import { messages, type Locale, type MessageTree } from './messages'
10: 
11: /**
12:  * 轻量 i18n。默认跟随系统语言（navigator.language），中文归一到 zh-CN，其余走 en。
13:  * t('a.b.c') 的调用签名与 i18next 一致，方便后续替换为 i18next 而不改调用点。
14:  * 详见 docs/modules/ui-theming-i18n.md。
15:  */
16: 
17: const STORAGE_KEY = 'deva.locale'
18: const SUPPORTED: Locale[] = ['zh-CN', 'en']
19: 
20: interface I18nContextValue {
21:   locale: Locale
22:   setLocale: (locale: Locale) => Promise<void>
23:   t: (key: string) => string
24: }
25: 
26: const I18nContext = createContext<I18nContextValue | null>(null)
27: 
28: function detectLocale(): Locale {
29:   // 优先 ~/.deva/config.json（可手改），其次 localStorage 兜底，最后跟随系统语言
30:   try {
31:     const v = window.deva?.config?.getSync()?.locale
32:     if (typeof v === 'string' && SUPPORTED.includes(v as Locale)) return v as Locale
33:   } catch {
34:     /* config 不可用时回退 */
35:   }
36:   try {
37:     const stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)
38:     if (stored && SUPPORTED.includes(stored as Locale)) return stored as Locale
39:   } catch {
40:     /* ignore */
41:   }
42:   const sys = (navigator.language || 'en').toLowerCase()
43:   return sys.startsWith('zh') ? 'zh-CN' : 'en'
44: }
45: 
46: function resolve(tree: MessageTree, key: string): string {
47:   const parts = key.split('.')
48:   let cur: string | MessageTree = tree
49:   for (const p of parts) {
50:     if (typeof cur !== 'object' || cur === null || !(p in cur)) return key
51:     cur = cur[p]
52:   }
53:   return typeof cur === 'string' ? cur : key
54: }
55: 
56: export function I18nProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }): React.JSX.Element {
57:   const [locale, setLocaleState] = useState<Locale>(detectLocale)
58: 
59:   useEffect(() => {
60:     document.documentElement.setAttribute('lang', locale)
61:   }, [locale])
62: 
63:   const setLocale = async (next: Locale): Promise<void> => {
64:     await window.deva.config.set({ locale: next })
65:     setLocaleState(next)
66:     // 主存 ~/.deva/config.json；localStorage 作为兜底缓存
67:     try {
68:       localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, next)
69:     } catch {
70:       /* ignore */
71:     }
72:   }
73: 
74:   const value = useMemo<I18nContextValue>(() => {
75:     const dict = messages[locale]
76:     return { locale, setLocale, t: (key: string) => resolve(dict, key) }
77:   }, [locale])
78: 
79:   return <I18nContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</I18nContext.Provider>
80: }
81: 
82: export function useI18n(): I18nContextValue {
83:   const ctx = useContext(I18nContext)
84:   if (!ctx) throw new Error('useI18n 必须在 I18nProvider 内使用')
85:   return ctx
86: }

$ nl -ba /home/agent/shared_workspace/deva/apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/i18n/messages.ts
1: /**
2:  * 语言资源。轻量自研 i18n，key 采用点分命名，与 i18next 兼容，便于后续平滑迁移。
3:  * 新增语言只需在此追加一份同结构字典。
4:  */
5: 
6: export type Locale = 'zh-CN' | 'en'
7: 
8: export interface MessageTree {
9:   [key: string]: string | MessageTree
10: }
11: 
12: const zhCN: MessageTree = {
13:   app: { name: 'Deva', tagline: '' },
14:   activity: {
15:     chat: '对话',
16:     explorer: '资源管理器',
17:     git: '版本控制',
18:     extensions: '扩展',
19:     settings: '设置'
20:   },
21:   titlebar: {
22:     openWorkspace: '打开项目',
23:     openFolder: '打开文件夹…',
24:     closeProject: '关闭项目',
25:     search: '搜索',
26:     toggleSidebar: '切换侧边栏',
27:     togglePanel: '切换底部面板'
28:   },
29:   projtabs: {
30:     more: '打开的项目 / 最近项目',
31:     openLabel: '打开的项目'
32:   },
33:   panel: {

38: type SettingsSection = 'general' | 'models' | 'extensions' | 'about'

41: export type SettingsInit = { section?: SettingsSection; ext?: { kind: 'skill' | 'mcp'; id: string } }
42: 
43: export function Settings({ initial, onClose }: { initial?: SettingsInit; onClose: () => void }): React.JSX.Element {
44:   const { t } = useI18n()
45:   const [section, setSection] = useState<SettingsSection>(initial?.section ?? 'general')
46:   // 直达的扩展详情只在首次进入扩展页时生效；切到别的页再回来是普通的扩展列表。
47:   const [extTarget, setExtTarget] = useState(initial?.ext ?? null)
48:   const nav: Array<{ id: SettingsSection; icon: React.ReactNode; label: string }> = [
49:     { id: 'general', icon: <Cog size={15} />, label: t('settings.general') },
50:     { id: 'models', icon: <Brain size={15} />, label: t('settings.models') },
51:     { id: 'extensions', icon: <Puzzle size={15} />, label: t('activity.extensions') },
52:     { id: 'about', icon: <Info size={15} />, label: t('settings.about') }
53:   ]
54:   return (
55:     // 遮罩层不响应点击：设置弹框只能通过右上角关闭按钮手动关闭，避免误触丢失上下文
56:     <div className="cf-modal__backdrop">
57:       <div className="cf-modal" role="dialog" aria-label={t('cf.settings')}>
58:         <div className="cf-modal__head">
59:           <span className="cf-modal__title">{t('cf.settings')}</span>
60:           <button className="cf-modal__close" title={t('common.close')} onClick={onClose}>
61:             ✕
62:           </button>
63:         </div>
64:         <div className="cf-modal__body">
65:           <nav className="cf-nav">
66:             {nav.map((n) => (
67:               <button
68:                 key={n.id}
69:                 className={`cf-nav__item${section === n.id ? ' is-active' : ''}`}
70:                 onClick={() => {
71:                   setSection(n.id)
72:                   setExtTarget(null)
73:                 }}
74:               >
75:                 <span className="cf-nav__icon">{n.icon}</span>
76:                 {n.label}
77:               </button>
78:             ))}
79:           </nav>
80:           <div className="cf-pane">
81:             {section === 'general' && <GeneralPane />}
82:             {section === 'models' && <ModelSettings />}
83:             {section === 'extensions' && <ExtensionsPane initialOpen={extTarget} />}
84:             {section === 'about' && <AboutPane />}
85:           </div>
86:         </div>
87:       </div>
88:     </div>
89:   )
90: }
91: 
92: function GeneralPane(): React.JSX.Element {
93:   const { t, locale, setLocale } = useI18n()
94:   const { mode, setMode } = useTheme()
95:   const toast = useToast()
96:   const saveSetting = (save: () => Promise<void>): void => {
97:     void save().catch((error: unknown) => toast.show({
98:       variant: 'error', title: t('settings.saveFailed'), message: String(error)
99:     }))
100:   }
101:   // 关窗驻留托盘：仅 Windows 呈现（macOS/Linux 关窗语义不同，主进程亦仅 win32 默认驻留）。
102:   const isWin = window.deva?.platform === 'win32'
103:   const [closeToTray, setCloseToTray] = useState<boolean>(() => {
104:     const v = window.deva?.config?.getSync?.().closeToTray
105:     return typeof v === 'boolean' ? v : true // win32 默认驻留（与主进程 close 判定一致）
106:   })
107:   const toggleTray = (): void => {
108:     const next = !closeToTray
109:     saveSetting(async () => {
110:       await window.deva.config.set({ closeToTray: next })
111:       setCloseToTray(next)
112:     })
113:   }
114:   // 文字大小：点选即生效（写 <html data-chat-fs>）并落盘，见 theme/chatFontSize.ts
115:   const [chatFontSize, setChatFontSize] = useState<ChatFontSize>(readChatFontSize)
116:   const pickChatFontSize = (v: string): void => {
117:     const next = v as ChatFontSize
118:     saveSetting(async () => {
119:       await saveChatFontSize(next)
120:       setChatFontSize(next)
121:     })
122:   }
123:   return (
124:     <>
125:       <h2 className="cf-pane__title">{t('settings.general')}</h2>
126:       <div className="cf-set">
127:         <div className="cf-set__row">
128:           <div className="cf-set__label">{t('settings.language')}</div>
129:           <Segmented
130:             value={locale}
131:             onChange={(v) => saveSetting(() => setLocale(v as 'zh-CN' | 'en'))}
132:             options={[
133:               { v: 'zh-CN', label: '简体中文' },
134:               { v: 'en', label: 'English' }
135:             ]}
136:           />
137:         </div>
138:         <div className="cf-set__row">
139:           <div className="cf-set__label">
140:             {t('settings.theme')}
141:           </div>
142:           <Segmented
143:             value={mode}
144:             onChange={(v) => saveSetting(() => setMode(v as 'light' | 'dark' | 'system'))}
145:             options={[
146:               { v: 'system', label: t('settings.themeSystem') },
147:               { v: 'light', label: t('settings.themeLight') },
148:               { v: 'dark', label: t('settings.themeDark') }
149:             ]}
150:           />
151:         </div>
152:         <div className="cf-set__row">
153:           <div className="cf-set__label">{t('settings.chatFontSize')}</div>
154:           <Segmented
155:             value={chatFontSize}
156:             onChange={pickChatFontSize}
157:             options={[
158:               { v: 'sm', label: t('settings.chatFontSm') },
159:               { v: 'md', label: t('settings.chatFontMd') },
160:               { v: 'lg', label: t('settings.chatFontLg') },
161:               { v: 'xl', label: t('settings.chatFontXl') }
162:             ]}
163:           />
164:         </div>
165:         {isWin && (
166:           <div className="cf-set__row">
167:             <div className="cf-set__label">
168:               {t('settings.closeToTray')}
169:             </div>
170:             <Toggle on={closeToTray} onChange={toggleTray} />
171:           </div>
172:         )}
173:       </div>
174:     </>
175:   )
176: }
177: 
178: function ExtensionsPane({
179:   initialOpen
180: }: {
181:   initialOpen?: { kind: 'skill' | 'mcp'; id: string } | null
182: }): React.JSX.Element {
183:   const { t } = useI18n()
184:   // 角色（persona）不在此列出：它有专属的「角色」tab 与资料卡来管理，扩展页只管技能 / MCP。
185:   // 子智能体亦不在此列：它是内置能力（通用 / Explore / Research / Plan），不可配置，见 main/services/subagents.ts。
186:   const { skills, mcp, toggle, refresh } = useExtensions()
187:   // 进入扩展页即从磁盘重拉最新：技能平时由主进程目录监听实时推送，这里兜底监听失效（如技能目录在网络盘）
188:   // 并顺带刷新 MCP。refresh 标识稳定，不会形成刷新循环。
189:   useEffect(() => {
190:     refresh()
191:   }, [refresh])
192: 
193:   // 钻取导航：点开某行 → 进入详情（对齐模型设置页的主从抽屉）。MCP 详情可编辑；技能详情只读，
194:   // 只提供删除与启停；内置技能不展示详情。新增经对话工具 / 上传 / 直接改盘，扩展页不放新增入口。
195:   const [open, setOpen] = useState<{ kind: 'skill' | 'mcp'; id: string } | null>(initialOpen ?? null)
196:   const openMcp = open?.kind === 'mcp' ? mcp.find((m) => m.id === open.id) : undefined
197:   const openSkill = open?.kind === 'skill' ? skills.find((s) => s.id === open.id) : undefined
198: 
199:   if (openMcp) {
200:     return <McpEditor key={openMcp.id} item={openMcp} onBack={() => setOpen(null)} />
201:   }
202:   if (openSkill) {
203:     return <SkillDetail item={openSkill} onBack={() => setOpen(null)} />
204:   }
205: 
206:   type Item = {
207:     kind: 'skill' | 'mcp'
208:     id: string
209:     name: string
210:     enabled: boolean
211:     badge: string
212:     note?: string
213:     /** 注记以警示色显示（MCP 已启用却没连上：失败 / 待填密钥 / 待完善配置）。 */
214:     noteWarn?: boolean
215:     /** 可用角色（null = 所有角色，行尾不显示；内置技能不设）。 */
216:     roles?: string[] | null
217:     locked?: boolean
218:   }
219:   const items: Item[] = [
220:     ...skills.map(
221:       (s): Item => ({
222:         kind: 'skill',
223:         id: s.id,
224:         name: s.name,
225:         enabled: s.enabled,
226:         badge: t('cf.kindSkill'),
227:         note: s.source === 'builtin' ? t('cf.builtin') : undefined,
228:         roles: s.source === 'builtin' ? undefined : s.roles,
229:         locked: s.source === 'builtin'
230:       })
231:     ),
232:     ...mcp.map(
233:       (m): Item => ({
234:         kind: 'mcp',
235:         id: m.id,
236:         name: m.name,
237:         enabled: m.enabled,
238:         badge: t('cf.kindMcp'),
239:         // 未启用不加注记（开关本身已表达）；已启用则如实给出连接状态，没连上的原因用警示色。
240:         note: m.enabled ? t(`extensions.status.${m.status}`) : undefined,
241:         noteWarn: m.enabled && MCP_STATUS_WARN.has(m.status),
242:         roles: m.roles
243:       })
244:     )
245:   ]
246:   return (
247:     <>
248:       <h2 className="cf-pane__title">{t('activity.extensions')}</h2>
249:       {items.length === 0 ? (
250:         <div className="cf-empty">{t('cf.extEmpty')}</div>
251:       ) : (
252:         <div className="cf-rows">
253:           {items.map((x) => {
254:             const name = (
255:               <span className="cf-setrow__name">
256:                 <span className="cf-setrow__label" title={x.name}>
257:                   {x.name}
258:                 </span>
259:                 <span className="cf-badge">{x.badge}</span>
260:                 {x.note && (
261:                   <span className={`cf-setrow__note${x.noteWarn ? ' cf-setrow__note--warn' : ''}`}>
262:                     {x.note}
263:                   </span>
264:                 )}
265:               </span>
266:             )
267:             return (
268:               <div key={`${x.kind}:${x.id}`} className="cf-setrow">
269:                 {x.locked ? (
270:                   // 内置技能不展示详情：只读一行，开关禁用。
271:                   <div className="cf-setrow__main">{name}</div>
272:                 ) : (
273:                   // 主区做按钮（点开详情），开关作兄弟节点独立触发（互不干扰）。
274:                   <button className="cf-setrow__open" onClick={() => setOpen({ kind: x.kind, id: x.id })}>
275:                     {name}
276:                     {x.roles !== undefined && <RolesBadge roles={x.roles} />}
277:                     <ChevronRight className="cf-setrow__chevron" size={15} />
278:                   </button>
279:                 )}
280:                 <Toggle on={x.enabled} disabled={x.locked} onChange={() => toggle(x.kind, x.id)} />
281:               </div>
282:             )
283:           })}
284:         </div>
285:       )}
286:     </>
287:   )
288: }
289: 
290: /** 可用角色里还存在的角色（按花名册顺序）；null = 所有角色。 */
291: function usePickedPersonas(roles: string[] | null): Persona[] {
292:   const { personas } = useExtensions()
293:   return useMemo(() => (roles === null ? [] : personas.filter((p) => roles.includes(p.id))), [personas, roles])
294: }
295: 
296: /**
297:  * 扩展列表行尾的可用角色：指定了角色显示头像堆叠，所有角色不显示。指定的角色都删了时主进程会把该项
298:  * 改回所有角色并停用（personas.ts pruneExtRoles），渲染层不再有「无可用角色」这一态。
299:  */
300: function RolesBadge({ roles }: { roles: string[] | null }): React.JSX.Element | null {
301:   const picked = usePickedPersonas(roles)
302:   return picked.length ? <AvatarStack personas={picked} /> : null
303: }
304: 
305: /**
306:  * 技能 / MCP 详情的「可用角色」：统一下拉框的多选模式。收起时显示「所有角色」或所选角色的头像堆叠与名字；
307:  * 浮层第一项「所有角色」（点了即收起），其下「只给这些角色」逐个勾选，即时生效。从所有角色点某个角色即只给它，
308:  * 取消到一个不剩即回到所有角色。
309:  */
310: /** 「所有角色」选项的值：角色 id 只含字母数字与 _.-，不会与之相撞。 */
311: const ROLES_GENERAL = '*'
312: 
313: function RolesPicker({
314:   kind,
315:   id,
316:   roles
317: }: {
318:   kind: 'skill' | 'mcp'
319:   id: string
320:   roles: string[] | null
321: }): React.JSX.Element {
322:   const { t, locale } = useI18n()
323:   const { personas, setRoles } = useExtensions()
324:   const picked = usePickedPersonas(roles)
325:   // 没有指定（或指定的角色刚被删、主进程正改回所有角色）都按所有角色显示。
326:   const all = picked.length === 0
327:   const sep = locale === 'en' ? ', ' : '、'
328: 
329:   const toggle = (pid: string): void => {
330:     if (pid === ROLES_GENERAL) {
331:       if (roles !== null) setRoles(kind, id, null)
332:       return
333:     }
334:     if (all) return setRoles(kind, id, [pid])
335:     const live = picked.map((p) => p.id)
336:     const next = live.includes(pid) ? live.filter((x) => x !== pid) : [...live, pid]
337:     setRoles(kind, id, next.length ? next : null)
338:   }
339: 
340:   const summary =
341:     picked.length <= 2
342:       ? picked.map((p) => p.name).join(sep)
343:       : t('extensions.rolesSummary')
344:           .replace('{names}', picked
345:             .slice(0, 2)
346:             .map((p) => p.name)
347:             .join(sep))
348:           .replace('{n}', String(picked.length))
349:           .replace('{rest}', String(picked.length - 2))
350: 
351:   const display = all ? (
352:     <>
353:       <span className="select__lead">
354:         <span className="select-icon">
355:           <Users size={12} />
356:         </span>
357:       </span>
358:       <span className="select__label">{t('extensions.rolesGeneral')}</span>
359:     </>
360:   ) : (
361:     <>
362:       <span className="select__lead">
363:         <AvatarStack personas={picked} size={20} />
364:       </span>
365:       <span className="select__label">{summary}</span>
366:     </>
367:   )
368: 
369:   return (
370:     <div className="ext-field">
371:       <label className="ext-field__label">{t('extensions.roles')}</label>
372:       <Select
373:         multiple
374:         aria-label={t('extensions.roles')}
375:         display={display}
376:         isSelected={(v) => (v === ROLES_GENERAL ? all : picked.some((p) => p.id === v))}
377:         onToggle={toggle}
378:         items={[
379:           {
380:             value: ROLES_GENERAL,
381:             label: t('extensions.rolesGeneral'),
382:             sub: t('extensions.rolesGeneralHint'),
383:             // 「所有角色」是一个整体，点了同单选一样收起；只有具体角色才多选。
384:             closeOnPick: true,
385:             lead: (
386:               <span className="select-icon">
387:                 <Users size={14} />
388:               </span>
389:             )
390:           },
391:           { separator: true },
392:           { group: t('extensions.rolesOnly') },
393:           ...personas.map((p) => ({
394:             value: p.id,
395:             label: p.name,
396:             sub: p.desc || undefined,
397:             lead: (
398:               <span className="select-ava">
399:                 <PersonaFace seed={p.id} spec={parseAvatarSpec(p.avatar)} image={p.avatarImage} />
400:               </span>
401:             )
402:           }))
403:         ]}
404:       />
405:     </div>
406:   )
407: }
408: 
409: /**
410:  * 技能详情（对话优先外壳）：只读的钻取式主从抽屉，顶栏与名称分隔线同 MCP 详情（返回 · 删除 · 启用）。
411:  * 只展示名称、可用角色与 SKILL.md 正文；描述及其余 frontmatter 字段（compatibility / license / metadata /
412:  * allowed-tools，以及 Deva 自有的 trigger）一律不显示。整页撑满设置面板、不出外层滚动条：
413:  * 正文吃掉剩余高度、过长时框内滚动。删除 = 技能文件夹移到系统回收站。
414:  * 仅自定义技能可进入；内置技能在列表里不可点开。
415:  */
416: function SkillDetail({ item, onBack }: { item: Skill; onBack: () => void }): React.JSX.Element {
417:   const { t, locale } = useI18n()
418:   const dialog = useDialog()
419:   const toast = useToast()
420:   const { toggle, removeSkill } = useExtensions()
421: 
422:   const onDelete = (): void => {
423:     const name = clipName(item.name)
424:     void (async () => {
425:       const ok = await dialog.confirm({
426:         title: locale === 'en' ? `Delete skill “${name}”?` : `删除技能 ${name} ？`,
427:         message: t(IS_MAC ? 'cf.skillDeleteConfirmMac' : 'cf.skillDeleteConfirm'),
428:         confirmText: t('cf.delete'),
429:         variant: 'danger'
430:       })
431:       if (!ok) return
432:       if (await removeSkill(item.id)) onBack()
433:       else toast.show({ variant: 'error', message: t('cf.skillDeleteFailed') })
434:     })()
435:   }
436: 
437:   return (
438:     <section className="provider-detail provider-detail--fill" key={item.id}>
439:       <div className="provider-detail__topbar">
440:         <button className="provider-detail__back" onClick={onBack}>
441:           <ChevronLeft size={16} />
442:           {t('common.back')}
443:         </button>
444:         <div className="provider-detail__spacer" />
445:         <button className="icon-btn provider-detail__del" title={t('extensions.remove')} onClick={onDelete}>
446:           <Trash2 size={15} />
447:         </button>
448:         <span className="provider-detail__enable">{t('extensions.enable')}</span>
449:         <Toggle on={item.enabled} onChange={() => toggle('skill', item.id)} />
450:       </div>
451:       <header className="provider-detail__head">
452:         <span className="ext-detail__icon">
453:           <Puzzle size={18} />
454:         </span>
455:         <h2 className="ext-detail__name ext-detail__name--mono">{item.name}</h2>
456:       </header>
457: 
458:       <RolesPicker kind="skill" id={item.id} roles={item.roles} />
459: 
460:       <div className="ext-field ext-field--grow">
461:         <label className="ext-field__label">{t('extensions.instructions')}</label>
462:         {item.instructions ? (
463:           <div className="ext-detail__md">
464:             <Markdown text={item.instructions} />
465:           </div>
466:         ) : (
467:           <span className="ext-detail__muted">{t('extensions.instructionsEmpty')}</span>
468:         )}
469:       </div>
470:     </section>
471:   )
472: }
473: 
474: /** 详情页运行期状态字形（● 连接 / ! 错或待处理 / ○ 未启用）。 */
475: const MCP_STATUS_GLYPH: Record<McpStatus, string> = {
476:   connected: '●',
477:   connecting: '●',
478:   error: '!',
479:   needs_config: '!',
480:   needs_secret: '!',
481:   disconnected: '○'
482: }
483: 
484: /** 已启用却没连上、需要用户处理的状态（列表注记用警示色）。 */
485: const MCP_STATUS_WARN = new Set<McpStatus>(['error', 'needs_config', 'needs_secret'])
486: 
487: /**
488:  * MCP 详情编辑（对话优先外壳）：钻取式主从抽屉，形态对齐模型设置页（顶部返回 + 逐字段即时落盘）。
489:  * 传输 / 命令 / 参数 / 环境变量（或 URL / 请求头）经 update('mcp', …) round-trip 到 mcp.json；名称只读
490:  * （创建时定下，见 create_mcp：改名不改 id、也不刷新已发给模型的工具说明，故不提供）。
491:  * 密钥恒经 mcpSetSecret 加密另存、写后不回显（`{secretRef}` 占位符落盘，明文零留痕）。
492:  */
493: function McpEditor({ item, onBack }: { item: McpServer; onBack: () => void }): React.JSX.Element {
494:   const { t } = useI18n()
495:   const dialog = useDialog()
496:   const toast = useToast()
497:   const { update, remove, toggle, mcpRetry, mcpSetSecret } = useExtensions()
498:   const [secretWarn, setSecretWarn] = useState(false)
499:   const isStdio = item.transport === 'stdio'
500:   const save = (patch: Partial<McpServer>): Promise<boolean> =>
501:     update('mcp', item.id, patch) ?? Promise.resolve(false)
502:   const command = useMcpTextDraft(item.command, (value) => save({ command: value }))
503:   const url = useMcpTextDraft(item.url, (value) => save({ url: value }))
504: 
505:   // 写入密钥字段：加密不可用时亮出降级提示（空值即删除已存密钥）。
506:   // 返回落盘结果，供 McpKvEditor 呈现「保存中 / 已保存」反馈。
507:   const setSecret = (field: string, value: string): Promise<{ ok: boolean; available: boolean }> =>
508:     mcpSetSecret(item.id, field, value).then((r) => {
509:       if (value && !r.available) setSecretWarn(true)
510:       else if (!r.ok) toast.show({ variant: 'error', message: t('settings.saveFailed') })
511:       return r
512:     })
513: 
514:   const onDelete = (): void => {
515:     void (async () => {
516:       const ok = await dialog.confirm({
517:         title: item.name,
518:         message: t('cf.mcpDeleteConfirm'),
519:         confirmText: t('common.delete'),
520:         variant: 'danger'
521:       })
522:       if (ok && await remove('mcp', item.id)) onBack()
523:     })()
524:   }
525: 
526:   return (
527:     <section className="provider-detail" key={item.id}>
528:       {/* 顶部工具条「返回 + 删除/启用」同一行，对齐模型设置页 */}
529:       <div className="provider-detail__topbar">
530:         <button className="provider-detail__back" onClick={onBack}>
531:           <ChevronLeft size={16} />
532:           {t('common.back')}
533:         </button>
534:         <div className="provider-detail__spacer" />
535:         <button className="icon-btn provider-detail__del" title={t('extensions.remove')} onClick={onDelete}>
536:           <Trash2 size={15} />
537:         </button>
538:         <span className="provider-detail__enable">{t('extensions.enable')}</span>
539:         <Toggle on={item.enabled} onChange={() => toggle('mcp', item.id)} />
540:       </div>
541:       <header className="provider-detail__head">
542:         <span className="ext-detail__icon">
543:           <Plug size={18} />
544:         </span>
545:         <h2 className="ext-detail__name ext-detail__name--mono ext-detail__name--clip" title={item.name}>
546:           {item.name}
547:         </h2>
548:         {/* 启用即连接：状态只读展示在标题行最右侧，唯一的动作是失败后「重试」。未启用一律显示「未启用」。 */}
549:         <span className={`ext-status__live ext-status__live--${item.enabled ? item.status : 'disconnected'}`}>
550:           {item.enabled
551:             ? `${MCP_STATUS_GLYPH[item.status]} ${t(`extensions.status.${item.status}`)}`
552:             : `${MCP_STATUS_GLYPH.disconnected} ${t('extensions.status.off')}`}
553:           {item.enabled && item.status === 'connected' && item.toolCount > 0
554:             ? ` · ${item.toolCount} ${t('extensions.tools')}`
555:             : ''}
556:         </span>
557:         {item.enabled && item.status === 'error' && (
558:           <button className="ext-linkbtn" onClick={() => mcpRetry(item.id)}>
559:             {t('extensions.retry')}
560:           </button>
561:         )}
562:       </header>
563:       {item.enabled && item.status === 'error' && item.lastError && (
564:         <div className="mcp-error">{item.lastError}</div>
565:       )}
566:       {item.enabled && item.status === 'needs_config' && item.lastError && (
567:         <div className="mcp-error mcp-error--warn">{item.lastError}</div>
568:       )}
569:       {item.enabled && item.status === 'needs_secret' && (
570:         <div className="mcp-error mcp-error--warn">
571:           {t('extensions.missingSecrets').replace('{fields}', item.missingSecrets.join(', '))}
572:         </div>
573:       )}
574: 
575:       <RolesPicker kind="mcp" id={item.id} roles={item.roles} />
576: 
577:       <div className="ext-field">
578:         <label className="ext-field__label">{t('extensions.transport')}</label>
579:         <Select<McpServer['transport']>
580:           mono
581:           aria-label={t('extensions.transport')}
582:           value={item.transport}
583:           onChange={(transport) => void save({ transport })}
584:           items={[
585:             { value: 'stdio', label: 'stdio', sub: t('extensions.transportSub.stdio') },
586:             { value: 'sse', label: 'sse', sub: t('extensions.transportSub.sse') },
587:             { value: 'http', label: 'http (streamable)', sub: t('extensions.transportSub.http') }
588:           ]}
589:         />
590:       </div>
591: 
592:       {isStdio ? (
593:         <>
594:           <div className="ext-field">
595:             <label className="ext-field__label">{t('extensions.command')}</label>
596:             <input
597:               className="ext-input ext-input--mono"
598:               placeholder={t('extensions.commandPlaceholder')}
599:               value={command.value}
600:               onChange={(e) => command.setDraft(e.target.value)}
601:               onBlur={command.commit}
602:             />
603:           </div>
604:           <McpArgsField item={item} />
605:           <div className="ext-field">
606:             <label className="ext-field__label">{t('extensions.env')}</label>
607:             <McpKvEditor
608:               serverId={item.id}
609:               rows={item.env}
610:               onChange={(rows) => save({ env: rows })}
611:               onSetSecret={setSecret}
612:             />
613:           </div>
614:         </>
615:       ) : (
616:         <>
617:           <div className="ext-field">
618:             <label className="ext-field__label">{t('extensions.url')}</label>
619:             <input
620:               className="ext-input ext-input--mono"
621:               placeholder={t('extensions.urlPlaceholder')}
622:               value={url.value}
623:               onChange={(e) => url.setDraft(e.target.value)}
624:               onBlur={url.commit}
625:             />
626:           </div>
627:           <div className="ext-field">
628:             <label className="ext-field__label">{t('extensions.headers')}</label>
629:             <McpKvEditor
630:               serverId={item.id}
631:               rows={item.headers}
632:               onChange={(rows) => save({ headers: rows })}
633:               onSetSecret={setSecret}
634:             />
635:           </div>
636:         </>
637:       )}
638: 
639:       {secretWarn && (
640:         <p className="ext-field__note ext-field__note--warn">{t('extensions.secretUnavailable')}</p>
641:       )}
642: 
643:       <div className="ext-field">
644:         <label className="ext-field__label">{t('extensions.discoveredTools')}</label>
645:         {item.tools.length === 0 ? (
646:           <div className="ext-tools--empty">{t('extensions.noToolsYet')}</div>
647:         ) : (
648:           <ul className="mcp-tools">
649:             {item.tools.map((tool) => (
650:               <li key={tool.name} className="mcp-tools__item">
651:                 <code className="mcp-tools__name">{tool.name}</code>
652:                 {tool.description && <span className="mcp-tools__desc">{tool.description}</span>}
653:               </li>
654:             ))}
655:           </ul>
656:         )}
657:       </div>
658:     </section>
659:   )
660: }
661: 
662: /** 命令参数编辑：一行一个（本地字符串态，随服务切换 remount 自动重置）。 */
663: function McpArgsField({ item }: { item: McpServer }): React.JSX.Element {
664:   const { t } = useI18n()
665:   const { update } = useExtensions()
666:   const args = useMcpTextDraft(item.args.join('\n'), (value) =>
667:     update('mcp', item.id, { args: value.split('\n').map((a) => a.trim()).filter(Boolean) }) ?? Promise.resolve(false)
668:   )
669:   return (
670:     <div className="ext-field">
671:       <label className="ext-field__label">{t('extensions.args')}</label>
672:       <textarea
673:         className="ext-area ext-area--mono"
674:         rows={3}
675:         placeholder={t('extensions.argsPlaceholder')}
676:         value={args.value}
677:         onChange={(e) => args.setDraft(e.target.value)}
678:         onBlur={args.commit}
679:       />
680:     </div>
681:   )
682: }
683: 
684: /**
685:  * 键值对编辑（env / headers）：明文失焦提交；密钥经 onSetSecret 加密。
686:  * 密钥字段对齐模型页 API-Key 交互：
687:  *  - 输入后**保留**遮罩草稿在框内（不再失焦即清空 → 不会「自动消失」）；
688:  *  - 防抖 ~600ms 自动保存 + 失焦 / 回车立即冲刷，落盘结果亮「保存中 / 已保存」；
689:  *  - 重新打开时草稿为空，据 hasSecret 亮「已配置」并提示「留空不修改」——始终不回显明文。
690:  */
691: function McpKvEditor({
692:   serverId,
693:   rows: savedRows,
694:   onChange,
695:   onSetSecret
696: }: {
697:   serverId: string
698:   rows: McpKV[]
699:   onChange: (rows: McpKV[]) => Promise<boolean>
700:   onSetSecret: (field: string, value: string) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; available: boolean }>
701: }): React.JSX.Element {
702:   const { t } = useI18n()
703:   const [rowDrafts, setRowDrafts] = useState<McpKV[] | null>(null)
704:   const rows = rowDrafts ?? savedRows
705:   const [drafts, setDrafts] = useState<Record<number, string>>({})
706:   const [saveState, setSaveState] = useState<Record<number, 'idle' | 'saving' | 'saved'>>({})
707:   const [configured, setConfigured] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({})
708:   const timersRef = useRef<Record<number, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>>({})
709: 
710:   // 各密钥行是否已配置（布尔，不回显明文）；键集变化时重查，点亮「已配置」。
711:   const secretSig = JSON.stringify(rows.map((r) => (r.secret ? r.key.trim() : '')))
712:   useEffect(() => {
713:     const keys = JSON.parse(secretSig) as string[]
714:     let alive = true
715:     void (async () => {
716:       const next: Record<number, boolean> = {}
717:       await Promise.all(
718:         keys.map(async (key, i) => {
719:           if (key) next[i] = (await window.deva?.mcp?.hasSecret?.(serverId, key)) ?? false
720:         })
721:       )
722:       if (alive) setConfigured(next)
723:     })()
724:     return () => {
725:       alive = false
726:     }
727:   }, [serverId, secretSig])
728: 
729:   const rowPatch = (i: number, patch: Partial<McpKV>): McpKV[] =>
730:     rows.map((r, idx) => (idx === i ? { ...r, ...patch } : r))
731:   const saveRows = async (next: McpKV[]): Promise<boolean> => {
732:     const valid = next.filter((r) => r.key.trim())
733:     const blanks = next.length !== valid.length
734:     setRowDrafts(next)
735:     const ok = JSON.stringify(valid) === JSON.stringify(savedRows) || await onChange(valid)
736:     setRowDrafts((current) => current === next ? (ok && blanks ? next : null) : current)
737:     return ok
738:   }
739:   const addRow = (): void => setRowDrafts([...rows, { key: '', value: '', secret: false }])
740:   const removeRow = (i: number): void => { void saveRows(rows.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i)) }
741: 
742:   const clearTimer = (i: number): void => {
743:     const tm = timersRef.current[i]
744:     if (tm) {
745:       clearTimeout(tm)
746:       timersRef.current[i] = null
747:     }
748:   }
749: 
750:   const doSaveSecret = async (i: number, key: string, value: string): Promise<void> => {
751:     if (!key || !value) return
752:     setSaveState((s) => ({ ...s, [i]: 'saving' }))
753:     const r = await onSetSecret(key, value)
754:     setSaveState((s) => ({ ...s, [i]: r.ok ? 'saved' : 'idle' }))
755:     if (r.ok) setConfigured((c) => ({ ...c, [i]: true }))
756:   }
757: 
758:   // 输入变化：更新草稿并防抖自动保存（不清空草稿，遮罩值留在框内）。
759:   const onSecretChange = (i: number, value: string): void => {
760:     setDrafts((d) => ({ ...d, [i]: value }))
761:     setSaveState((s) => ({ ...s, [i]: 'idle' }))
762:     clearTimer(i)
763:     const key = rows[i].key.trim()
764:     if (!key || !value) return
765:     timersRef.current[i] = setTimeout(() => {
766:       timersRef.current[i] = null
767:       void doSaveSecret(i, key, value)
768:     }, 600)
769:   }
770: 
771:   // 立即冲刷在途保存（失焦 / 回车）。
772:   const flushSecret = (i: number): void => {
773:     clearTimer(i)
774:     void doSaveSecret(i, rows[i].key.trim(), drafts[i] ?? '')
775:   }
776: 
777:   const toggleSecret = async (i: number): Promise<void> => {
778:     const r = rows[i]
779:     const key = r.key.trim()
780:     clearTimer(i)
781:     if (!await saveRows(rowPatch(i, { secret: !r.secret, value: '' }))) return
782:     if (r.secret && key) await onSetSecret(key, '') // 仅在配置保存后清除旧密钥
783:     setDrafts((d) => {
784:       const n = { ...d }
785:       delete n[i]
786:       return n
787:     })
788:     setSaveState((s) => {
789:       const n = { ...s }
790:       delete n[i]
791:       return n
792:     })
793:     setConfigured((c) => {
794:       const n = { ...c }
795:       delete n[i]
796:       return n
797:     })
798:   }
799: 
800:   return (
801:     <div className="kv-editor">
802:       {rows.map((r, i) => (
803:         <div className="kv-row-wrap" key={i}>
804:           <div className="kv-row">
805:             <input
806:               className="ext-input ext-input--mono kv-row__key"
807:               placeholder={t('extensions.kvKey')}
808:               value={r.key}
809:               onChange={(e) => setRowDrafts(rowPatch(i, { key: e.target.value }))}
810:               onBlur={() => { if (rowDrafts) void saveRows(rowDrafts) }}
811:             />
812:             {r.secret ? (
813:               <input
814:                 className="ext-input ext-input--mono kv-row__val"
815:                 type="password"
816:                 placeholder={
817:                   configured[i]
818:                     ? t('extensions.secretPlaceholder')
819:                     : t('extensions.secretValuePlaceholder')
820:                 }
821:                 value={drafts[i] ?? ''}
822:                 onChange={(e) => onSecretChange(i, e.target.value)}
823:                 onBlur={() => flushSecret(i)}
824:                 onKeyDown={(e) => {
825:                   if (e.key === 'Enter') flushSecret(i)
826:                 }}
827:               />
828:             ) : (
829:               <input
830:                 className="ext-input ext-input--mono kv-row__val"
831:                 placeholder={t('extensions.kvValue')}
832:                 value={r.value}
833:                 onChange={(e) => setRowDrafts(rowPatch(i, { value: e.target.value }))}
834:                 onBlur={() => { if (rowDrafts) void saveRows(rowDrafts) }}
835:               />
836:             )}
837:             <button
838:               className={`icon-btn icon-btn--sm kv-row__lock${r.secret ? ' is-on' : ''}`}
839:               title={r.secret ? t('extensions.unmarkSecret') : t('extensions.markSecret')}
840:               onClick={() => void toggleSecret(i)}
841:             >
842:               {r.secret ? <Lock size={13} /> : <Unlock size={13} />}
843:             </button>
844:             <button
845:               className="icon-btn icon-btn--sm"
846:               title={t('extensions.removeRow')}
847:               onClick={() => removeRow(i)}
848:             >
849:               <X size={13} />
850:             </button>
851:           </div>
852:           {r.secret && (saveState[i] === 'saving' || saveState[i] === 'saved' || configured[i]) && (
853:             <div className="kv-row__status">
854:               {saveState[i] === 'saving' ? (
855:                 <span className="key-status">{t('extensions.secretSaving')}</span>
856:               ) : saveState[i] === 'saved' ? (
857:                 <span className="key-status">
858:                   <ShieldCheck size={12} />
859:                   {t('extensions.secretSaved')}
860:                 </span>
861:               ) : (
862:                 <span className="key-status">
863:                   <ShieldCheck size={12} />
864:                   {t('extensions.secretConfigured')}
865:                 </span>
866:               )}
867:             </div>
868:           )}
869:         </div>
870:       ))}
871:       <button className="btn btn--ghost btn--sm kv-add" onClick={addRow}>
872:         <Plus size={13} /> {t('extensions.addRow')}
873:       </button>
874:     </div>
875:   )
876: }
877: 
878: function AboutPane(): React.JSX.Element {
879:   const { t } = useI18n()
880:   return (
881:     <>
882:       <h2 className="cf-pane__title">{t('settings.about')}</h2>
883:       <div className="cf-about">
884:         <img className="cf-about__icon" src={devaIcon} alt="" aria-hidden />
885:         <div className="cf-about__name">{t('app.name')}</div>
886:         <div className="cf-about__ver">
887:           {t('settings.version')} {__APP_VERSION__}
888:         </div>
889:         {t('app.tagline') && <p className="cf-about__desc">{t('app.tagline')}</p>}
890:         <UpdateSection />
891:       </div>
892:     </>
893:   )
894: }
